<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cube measuring $3 \ \text{cm} \times 3 \ \text{cm} \times 3 \ \text{cm}$ is made up of small cubes and has a
$1 \ \text{cm} \times 1 \ \text{cm}$ square hole pushed right through the centre of each face so that you can see straight through the cube from every side.

![papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/2019/sample-paper-1/s02-q22-i00.png](/assets/papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/2019/sample-paper-1/s02-q22-i00.png "papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/2019/sample-paper-1/s02-q22-i00.png")

How many small cubes are used to make this shape?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A cube measuring $3 \ \text{cm} \times 3 \ \text{cm} \times 3 \ \text{cm}$ is made up of small cubes and has a
$1 \ \text{cm} \times 1 \ \text{cm}$ square hole pushed right through the centre of each face so that you can see straight through the cube from every side.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
How many small cubes are used to make this shape?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smaller cubes without removing}   &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the big cube}} {\text{Volume of 1 small cube}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 3} {1 \times 1 \times 1} \\\\
                                        &= 27 \\\\
\text{Cubes to remove}                  &= \text{Cube on each face} + \text{Cube at the center} \\\\
                                        &= 6 + 1 \\\\
                                        &= 7 \\\\
\text{Cubes needed}                     &= 27 - 7 \\\\
                                        &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smaller cubes without removing}   &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the big cube}} {\text{Volume of 1 small cube}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 3} {1 \times 1 \times 1} \\\\
                                        &= 27 \\\\
\text{Cubes to remove}                  &= \text{Cube on each face} + \text{Cube at the center} \\\\
                                        &= 6 + 1 \\\\
                                        &= 7 \\\\
\text{Cubes needed}                     &= 27 - 7 \\\\
                                        &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Here are two containers and the amount they hold.

![papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i00.png](/assets/papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i00.png "papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i00.png")

Which container holds the greater amount?
- A
- B

How much more does it hold?

Give your answer in millilitres.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Here are two containers and the amount they hold.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Which container holds the greater amount?
- A
- B
<emptyline>
How much more does it hold?
<emptyline>
Give your answer in millilitres.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {A}           &= 750 \ \text {ml} \\\\
\text {B}           &= 0.5 \ \text {litre} \\\\
                    &= 0.5 \times 1000 \ \text {ml} \\\\
                    &= 500 \ \text {ml} \\\\
\text {Difference}  &= 750 - 500 \\\\
                    &= 250 \ \text {ml}
\end{aligned}
$

Container A holds $250 \ \text {ml}$ more than container B.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

A

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$250 \ \text{milliletres}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kaylee has some $1 \ \text{cm}$ cubes.

She makes a solid cube with side length $6 \ \text{cm}$ out of the cubes.

![papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q09-i00.png](/assets/papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q09-i00.png "papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q09-i00.png")

Then she uses all these cubes to make some cubes with side length $2 \ \text{cm}$.

How many of these $2 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can Kaylee make?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Kaylee has some $1 \ \text{cm}$ cubes.
<emptyline>
She makes a solid cube with side length $6 \ \text{cm}$ out of the cubes.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Then she uses all these cubes to make some cubes with side length $2 \ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
How many of these $2 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can Kaylee make?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of } 2 \text{ cm cubes}  &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the } 6 \text{ cm cube}} {\text{Volume of the } 2 \text{ cm cube}} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {6 \times 6 \times 6} {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 3} {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 3 \times \cancel{2} \times 3 \times \cancel{2} \times 3} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
                                      &= 3 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\
                                      &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$27$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the volume of one of the cubes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the volume of one of the cubes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of a cube}           &= \dfrac {875}{7}  \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {125 \times 7}{7}  \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {125 \times \cancel{7}}{\cancel{7}}  \\\\
                                  &= 125 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$125 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the length of one side of a cube?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the length of one side of a cube?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the cube $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a^3                     &= 125 \\\\
a                       &= \sqrt[3]{125} \\\\
a                       &= \sqrt[3]{5 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
a                       &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length $= 5 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

                                  &= \dfrac {125 \times 7}{7}  \\\\

                                  &= 125 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the length of one side of a cube?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the length of one side of a cube?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of the cube $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a^3                     &= 125 \\\\
a                       &= \sqrt[3]{125} \\\\
a                       &= \sqrt[3]{5 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
a                       &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length $= 5 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A $7 \times 7 \times 7$ cube is painted, and then cut into $1 \times 1 \times 1$ cubes. 

How many of these cubes are painted on exactly two sides?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A $7 \times 7 \times 7$ cube is painted, and then cut into $1 \times 1 \times 1$ cubes. 
<emptyline>
How many of these cubes are painted on exactly two sides?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes painted two sides}                &= (7 \times 7 - 6 \times 6) - 4 + (7 \times 7 - 6 \times 6) - 4 + 5 \times 4 \\\\
                                                        &= 2(49 - 36) - 8 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 26 - 8 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 38
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$38$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                                        &= 2(49 - 36) - 8 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 26 - 8 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 38
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Below are diagrams of a small cube and larger cuboid.
How many of the small cubes can be fully fitted into the larger cuboid?
All measurements are in centimetres.

![papers/chigwell-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png](/assets/papers/chigwell-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png "papers/chigwell-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Below are diagrams of a small cube and larger cuboid.
How many of the small cubes can be fully fitted into the larger cuboid?
All measurements are in centimetres.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of small cubes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of large cuboid}} {\text{Volume of small cube}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {18 \times 9 \times 9} {3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {6 \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \times 9} {3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3} \times 9} { \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
                       &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
                       &= 54 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                       &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3} \times 9} { \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\

$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A box measures $20\ \text{cm}$ by $11\ \text{cm}$ by $15\ \text{cm}$. Calculate the volume of the box.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume}  &= 20 \times 11 \times 15 \\\\
               &= 3300 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A string is tied around the box with a bow at the top as shown in the diagram.

If the length of the bow is $8\ \text{cm}$, calculate the total length of string used.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A string is tied around the box with a bow at the top as shown in the diagram.
<emptyline>
If the length of the bow is $8\ \text{cm}$, calculate the total length of string used.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total length of string}  &= 8 + 2 \times 11 +  4 \times 15 + 2 \times 20 \\\\
                               &= 8 + 22 + 60 + 40 \\\\
                               &= 130 \ \text{cm} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$130 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the largest number of $15\ \text{cm}$ by $30\ \text{cm}$ by $20\ \text{cm}$ shoe boxes that can fit in a $1\ \text{m} \times 3\ \text{m} \times 2\ \text{m}$ crate?

Be careful - you can't chop up the shoe boxes!

![papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q05-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q05-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q05-i00.png")       ![papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q05-i01.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q05-i01.png "papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q05-i01.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the largest number of $15\ \text{cm}$ by $30\ \text{cm}$ by $20\ \text{cm}$ shoe boxes that can fit in a $1\ \text{m} \times 3\ \text{m} \times 2\ \text{m}$ crate?
<emptyline>
Be careful - you can't chop up the shoe boxes!
<emptyline>
%image%       %image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of shoe boxes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the crate}} {\text{Volume of the shoe boxes}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.9 \ \text{m} \times 3 \ \text{m} \times 2 \ \text{m}}{15 \ \text{cm} \times 30 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {90 \ \text{cm} \times 300 \ \text{cm} \times 200 \ \text{cm}}{15 \ \text{cm} \times 30 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 10 \times 30 \times 10 \times 20 \times 10}{3 \times 5 \times 30 \times 20} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 3 \times 2 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{30} \times 10 \times \cancel{20} \times 10} {\cancel{3} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{30} \times \cancel{20}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 2 \times 10 \times 10 \\\\
                            &= 600
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$600$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of shoe boxes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the crate}} {\text{Volume of the shoe boxes}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.9 \ \text{m} \times 3 \ \text{m} \times 2 \ \text{m}}{15 \ \text{cm} \times 30 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {90 \ \text{cm} \times 300 \ \text{cm} \times 200 \ \text{cm}}{15 \ \text{cm} \times 30 \ \text{cm} \times 20 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 10 \times 30 \times 10 \times 20 \times 10}{3 \times 5 \times 30 \times 20} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 3 \times 2 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{30} \times 10 \times \cancel{20} \times 10} {\cancel{3} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{30} \times \cancel{20}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 2 \times 10 \times 10 \\\\
                            &= 600
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2020/sample-paper-5/s01-q27-i00.png](/assets/papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2020/sample-paper-5/s01-q27-i00.png "papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2020/sample-paper-5/s01-q27-i00.png")

What is the volume of the box above?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of the box} &= 3 \times 6 \times 3 \\\\
                         &= 54 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54 \ \text{cm}^3 $

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of the box} &= 3 \times 6 \times 3 \\\\
                         &= 54 \ \text{cm}^3

$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cube has a volume $12 \ \text{cm}^3$.

If the length of each edge is doubled, what will the new volume be?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A cube has a volume $12 \ \text{cm}^3$.
<emptyline>
If the length of each edge is doubled, what will the new volume be?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the edge of cube $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned} 
a^3                             &= 12 \\\\
\text{New volume}               &= (2a)^3 \\\\
                                &= 8a^3 \\\\
                                &= 8 \times 12 \\\\
                                &= 96 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$

New volume $= 96 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$96 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the edge of cube $= a$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$

\text{New volume}               &= (2a)^3 \\\\
                                &= 8a^3 \\\\
                                &= 8 \times 12 \\\\
                                &= 96 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
New volume $= 96 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the volume of a cuboid with length $8 \ \text{cm}$, width $5 \ \text{cm}$ and height $6 \ \text{cm}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the volume of a cuboid with length $8 \ \text{cm}$, width $5 \ \text{cm}$ and height $6 \ \text{cm}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Volume of a cuboid}    &= 8  \times 5  \times 6  \\\\
                              &= 40 \times 6 \\\\
                              &= 240 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$240 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Volume of a cuboid}    &= 8  \times 5  \times 6  \\\\
                              &= 40 \times 6 \\\\
                              &= 240 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the volume of a rectangular box which is $5 \ \text{cm}$ long, $20 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $10 \ \text{cm}$ high?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume}   &= 5 \  \times 20  \times 10  \\\\
                &= 1000 \ \text{cm}^{3}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1000 \ \text{cm}^{3}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many $5 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can be packed into the rectangular box in part a) ?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many $5 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can be packed into the rectangular box in part a) ?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes} &= \dfrac { \text{Volume of rectangular box} } { \text{Volume of cube} } \\\\
                       &= \dfrac { 5 \times 20 \times 10 } { 5 \times 5 \times 5  } \\\\
                       &= \dfrac { 5 \times 4 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5 } { 5 \times 5 \times 5  } \\\\
                       &= \dfrac { \cancel{5} \times 4 \times \cancel{5} \times 2 \times \cancel{5} } { \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}  } \\\\
                       &= 4 \times 2 \\\\
                       &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

                       &= \dfrac { \cancel{5} \times 4 \times \cancel{5} \times 2 \times \cancel{5} } { \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}  } \\\\

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the volume of a box of jelly beans. Give the units for your answer.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the volume of a box of jelly beans. Give the units for your answer.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of the box} &= 12 \times 10 \times 3 \\\\
                         &= 120 \times 3 \\\\
                         &= 360 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$360 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The boxes are packed into cartons which are cuboids measuring $60\ \text{cm}$ by $30\ \text{cm}$ by $12\ \text{cm}$.

Find the number of boxes of jelly beans which may be packed into a carton.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The boxes are packed into cartons which are cuboids measuring $60\ \text{cm}$ by $30\ \text{cm}$ by $12\ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
Find the number of boxes of jelly beans which may be packed into a carton.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of boxes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the carton}} {\text{Volume of the box}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {60 \times 30 \times 12} {10 \times 3 \times 12} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {60 \times 3 \times 10 \times 12} {10 \times 3 \times 12} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {60 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{10} \times \cancel{12}} {\cancel{10} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{12}} \\\\
                       &= 60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of boxes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the carton}} {\text{Volume of the box}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {60 \times 30 \times 12} {10 \times 3 \times 12} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {60 \times 3 \times 10 \times 12} {10 \times 3 \times 12} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {60 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{10} \times \cancel{12}} {\cancel{10} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{12}} \\\\
                       &= 60
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many $3 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can be made by cutting up a block $12 \ \text{cm} \times 24 \ \text{cm} \times 18 \ \text{cm}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many $3 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can be made by cutting up a block $12 \ \text{cm} \times 24 \ \text{cm} \times 18 \ \text{cm}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of block}} {\text{Volume of cube}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {12 \times 24 \times 18} {3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 3 \times 8 \times 6 \times 3} {3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 4 \times \cancel {3} \times 8 \times 6 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {3}} \\\\
                       &= 4 \times 8 \times 6 \\\\
                       &= 192
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$192$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A large cube is made by gluing together smaller white and grey cubes of volume $1 \ \text{cm}^3$ as shown. 
If five grey cubes are removed from each face of the larger cube, what is the total volume remaining?

![papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-4/s01-q23-i00.png](/assets/papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-4/s01-q23-i00.png "papers/merchant-taylors-school/sample/practice-paper-4/s01-q23-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A large cube is made by gluing together smaller white and grey cubes of volume $1 \ \text{cm}^3$ as shown. 
If five grey cubes are removed from each face of the larger cube, what is the total volume remaining?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of the big cube}       &= 5 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
                                    &= 125 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
\text{Volume of cubes to remove}    &= 6 \times 1 \times 1 \times 1 \\\\
                                    &= 6 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
\text{Remaining Volume}             &= 125 - 30 \\\\
                                    &= 95 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$95 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="uuid">
<p>2D9E6D0E-4E54-4B9F-A732-DB3E447B66D7</p>

<p>sevenoaks-school--11-plus--maths--2012--sample-paper-3:1:7</p>

<div class="rag">

tvolume
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Toby is going to have a party and fills his cool box with blocks of ice cream. 

The cool box is rectangular with dimensions $30 \ \text{cm} \times 30 \ \text{cm} \times 62 \ \text{cm}$. 
The ice cream blocks are each rectangular with dimensions $10 \ \text{cm} \times 5 \ \text{cm} \times 15 \ \text{cm}$.

How many blocks of ice-cream can he put into the cool box?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Toby is going to have a party and fills his cool box with blocks of ice cream. 
<emptyline>
The cool box is rectangular with dimensions $30 \ \text{cm} \times 30 \ \text{cm} \times 62 \ \text{cm}$. 
The ice cream blocks are each rectangular with dimensions $10 \ \text{cm} \times 5 \ \text{cm} \times 15 \ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
How many blocks of ice-cream can he put into the cool box?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of ice cream blocks}    &= \dfrac {\text {Volume of the cool box}} {\text {Volume of a ice cream block}} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {30 \times 30 \times 62}  {10 \times 5 \times 15} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 10 \times 2 \times 15 \times 62}  {10 \times 5 \times 15} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 2 \times \cancel{15} \times 62}  {1 \cancel{0} \times 5 \times \cancel{15}} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 62} {5} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {372} {5} \\\\
                                      &= 74 \dfrac {2} {5} \\\\
                                      &\approx 74
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$74$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\text {Number of ice cream blocks}    &= \dfrac {\text {Volume of the cool box}} {\text {Volume of a ice cream block}} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {30 \times 30 \times 62}  {10 \times 5 \times 15} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 10 \times 2 \times 15 \times 62}  {10 \times 5 \times 15} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 2 \times \cancel{15} \times 62}  {1 \cancel{0} \times 5 \times \cancel{15}} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 62} {5} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {372} {5} \\\\
                                      &= 74 \dfrac {2} {5} \\\\
                                      &\approx 74
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many $2 \times 2 \times 2 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can you fit into the box below?

![papers/sevenoaks-school/2013/sample-paper-4/s01-q11-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/sevenoaks-school/2013/sample-paper-4/s01-q11-s01-i00.png "papers/sevenoaks-school/2013/sample-paper-4/s01-q11-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many $2 \times 2 \times 2 \ \text{cm}$ cubes can you fit into the box below?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}  
\text{Number of small boxes that can fit}    &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the box}} {\text{Volume of the small box}} \\\\
                                             &= \dfrac {6 \times 10 \times 20} {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                                             &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 10 } {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                                             &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times 5 \times \cancel {2} \times 10} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                                             &= 3 \times 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                             &= 150
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$150$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much space is left if you pour half a litre of water into the box ($1 \ \text{litre}$ of water has a volume of $1000 \ \text{cm}^3$)?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much space is left if you pour half a litre of water into the box ($1 \ \text{litre}$ of water has a volume of $1000 \ \text{cm}^3$)?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Space left} &= \text{Volume of box} - \text{Volume of water pour} \\\\
                  &= 1200 \ \text{cm}^3 - \dfrac{1} {2} \ \text{litre} \\\\\
                  &= 1200 \ \text{cm}^3 - \dfrac{1} {2} \times 1000 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
                  &= 1200 - \dfrac {1000} {2} \\\\
                  &= 1200 - \dfrac {500 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                  &= 1200 - \dfrac {500 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                  &= 1200 - 500 \\\\
                  &= 700 \ \text{cm}^3 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$700 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                  &= 1200 \ \text{cm}^3 - \dfrac{1} {2} \ \text{litre} \\\\\
                  &= 1200 \ \text{cm}^3 - \dfrac{1} {2} \times 1000 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
                  &= 1200 - \dfrac {1000} {2} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A $7 \times 7 \times 7$ cube is painted, and then cut into $1 \times 1 \times 1$ cubes. How many of these cubes are painted on exactly two sides?

![papers/sevenoaks-school/2014/sample-paper-5/s01-q12-i00.png](/assets/papers/sevenoaks-school/2014/sample-paper-5/s01-q12-i00.png "papers/sevenoaks-school/2014/sample-paper-5/s01-q12-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A $7 \times 7 \times 7$ cube is painted, and then cut into $1 \times 1 \times 1$ cubes. How many of these cubes are painted on exactly two sides?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes painted two sides}                &= (7 \times 7 - 6 \times 6) - 4 + (7 \times 7 - 6 \times 6) - 4 + 5 \times 4 \\\\
                                                        &= 2(49 - 36) - 8 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 26 - 8 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 38
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$38$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A model house is made by sticking a triangular prism on top of a rectangular block as shown in the diagram. Find the volume of the model house.

![papers/sevenoaks-school/2016/sample-paper-7/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/sevenoaks-school/2016/sample-paper-7/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/sevenoaks-school/2016/sample-paper-7/s01-q17-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A model house is made by sticking a triangular prism on top of a rectangular block as shown in the diagram. Find the volume of the model house.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE



*TODO:: Add an image with the shape, joining to shape 0f prism ,by cutting another identical prism from center and putting it on both side of prism in a way that they form prism *


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


By symmetry the volume of prism is half of cuboid

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of cuboid}         &= 4 \times 3 \times 12.5 \\\\
                                &= 150 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
\text{Volume of prism}          &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{Volume of cuboid} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times 150 \\\\
                                &= 75 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
\text{NVolume of model house}   &= \text{Volume of cuboid} + \text{Volume of prism} \\\\
                                &= 150 + 75 \\\\
                                &= 225  \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$225  \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>

- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>

                                &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times 150 \\\\
                                &= 75 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the volume of the cube.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the volume of the cube.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Volume}            &= 5 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
                          &= 125 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$125 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the height of the cuboid.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the height of the cuboid.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the height of cuboid  $= h$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times 5 \times h      &= 125 \\\\
h                         &= \dfrac {125} {10 \times 5} \\\\
h                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 5 \times 5} {2 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
h                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {5}} {2 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
h                         &= \dfrac {5} {2} \\\\
h                         &= 2.5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2.5 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>

</code></pre>

All the cubes on all the faces will have atleast one red face. Ensure we do not count the cubes more than once.

                        &= 50 + 30 + 18 \\\\
                        &= 98
\end{aligned}
$

                                &= 36
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A company makes bricks to export to builders. To ship them, they put the bricks into packing cases. The lengths of the sides of the bricks and the cases are shown below. All
measurements are in $\text{cm}$.

![papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s02-q05-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s02-q05-i00.png "papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s02-q05-i00.png")

![papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s02-q05-i01.png](/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s02-q05-i01.png "papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s02-q05-i01.png")

What is the maximum number of bricks which can be fitted into the packing case?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A company makes bricks to export to builders. To ship them, they put the bricks into packing cases. The lengths of the sides of the bricks and the cases are shown below. All
measurements are in $\text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
What is the maximum number of bricks which can be fitted into the packing case?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Maximum number of blocks}                                                    &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of case}} {\text{Volume of brick}} \\\\
                                                                                   &= \dfrac {200 \times 180 \times 120} {30 \times 20 \times 15} \\\\
                                                                                   &= \dfrac {20 \times 2 \times 5 \times 30 \times 3 \times 2 \times 120} {30 \times 20 \times 5 \times 3} \\\\
                                                                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 2 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{30} \times \cancel{3} \times 2 \times 120} {\cancel{30} \times \cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
                                                                                   &= 2 \times 2 \times 120 \\\\
                                                                                   &= 480
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$480$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Maximum number of blocks}                                                    &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of case}} {\text{Volume of brick}} \\\\
                                                                                   &= \dfrac {200 \times 180 \times 120} {30 \times 20 \times 15} \\\\
                                                                                   &= \dfrac {20 \times 2 \times 5 \times 30 \times 3 \times 2 \times 120} {30 \times 20 \times 5 \times 3} \\\\
                                                                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 2 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{30} \times \cancel{3} \times 2 \times 120} {\cancel{30} \times \cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
                                                                                   &= 2 \times 2 \times 120 \\\\
                                                                                   &= 480
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<div class="papername">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i00.png "papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i00.png")

Blocks of this shape are packed into the container on the right. What is the greatest number of blocks that can be placed inside the container?

![papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i01.png](/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i01.png "papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s02-q12-i01.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
Blocks of this shape are packed into the container on the right. What is the greatest number of blocks that can be placed inside the container?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE



*TODO:: Add an image with the shape, joining to shape 0f block in a way that they form a cuboid*


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As shown in the diagram, we can fix 2 blocks in a way that they form a cuboid.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of cuboid}         &= 3 \times 1 \times 1\\\\
                                &= 3 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
\text{Number of cuboid}         &= \dfrac{\text{Volume of container}}{\text{Volume of cuboid}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{6 \times 2 \times 2}{3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{24}{3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{24}{3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{3 \times 8}{3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 8}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
                                &= 8 \\\\
\text{Number of blocks}         &= 2 \times \text{Number of cuboid} \\\\
                                &= 8 \times 2  \\\\
                                &= 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                &= \dfrac{6 \times 2 \times 2}{3} \\\\

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the difference in volumes of the two boxes?

![papers/stockport-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/stockport-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-s01-i00.png "papers/stockport-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the difference in volumes of the two boxes?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of cuboid} &= 6 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\
                        &=  36 \ \text{m}^3 \\\\
\text{Volume of cube}   &=  3 ^{3} \\\\
                        &=  27 \ \text{m}^3 \\\\
\text{Difference}       &=  36 - 27 \\\\
                        &=  9 \ \text{m}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9 \ \text{m}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What fraction of the bigger box's volume is the volume of the smaller box?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What fraction of the bigger box's volume is the volume of the smaller box?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction } &= \dfrac{\text{Volume of cube}}{\text{Volume of cuboid}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{27}{36} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{3 \times 9}{4 \times 9} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel 9}{4 \times \cancel 9} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{3}{4}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{3}{4}$

</div>
</div>
