**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let height of the cuboid $=h $

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
5 \times 2 \times h &= 40 \\\\
h &= \dfrac {40} {5 \times 2} \\\\\
h &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 4} {5 \times 2} \\\\\
h &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times \cancel{2} \times 4} {\cancel{5} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\\
h                               &=  4 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Height of the cuboid $=  4 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/sydenham-high-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q28-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/sydenham-high-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q28-s02-i00.png "papers/sydenham-high-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q28-s02-i00.png")

A carton measures $200 \ \text{cm}$ by $100 \ \text{cm}$ by $100 \ \text{cm}$.

The carton is to be completely filled with boxes.

Each box measures $50 \ \text{cm}$ by $20 \ \text{cm}$ by $20 \ \text{cm}$.

Work out the number of boxes which can completely fill the carton.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
A carton measures $200 \ \text{cm}$ by $100 \ \text{cm}$ by $100 \ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
The carton is to be completely filled with boxes.
<emptyline>
Each box measures $50 \ \text{cm}$ by $20 \ \text{cm}$ by $20 \ \text{cm}$.
<emptyline>
Work out the number of boxes which can completely fill the carton.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of boxes}  &= \dfrac {\text {Volume of carton} } { \text {Volume of a box} } \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {200 \times 100 \times 100} {50 \times 20 \times 20} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {50 \times 4 \times 20 \times 5 \times 20 \times 5} {50 \times 20 \times 20} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {\cancel{50} \times 4 \times \cancel{20} \times 5 \times \cancel{20} \times 5} {\cancel{50} \times \cancel{20} \times \cancel{20}} \\\\
                         &= 4 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
                         &= 100
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of boxes}  &= \dfrac {\text {Volume of carton} } { \text {Volume of a box} } \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {200 \times 100 \times 100} {50 \times 20 \times 20} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {50 \times 4 \times 20 \times 5 \times 20 \times 5} {50 \times 20 \times 20} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {\cancel{50} \times 4 \times \cancel{20} \times 5 \times \cancel{20} \times 5} {\cancel{50} \times \cancel{20} \times \cancel{20}} \\\\
                         &= 4 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
                         &= 100
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The corners of this rectangular sheet of cardboard are cut out and the sheet is then folded to make a box without a lid. 
Work out the volume of the box and state the units.

![papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2015/sample-paper-6/s01-q23-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2015/sample-paper-6/s01-q23-i00.png "papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2015/sample-paper-6/s01-q23-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The corners of this rectangular sheet of cardboard are cut out and the sheet is then folded to make a box without a lid. 
Work out the volume of the box and state the units.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Length}   &= 26 - 2 \times 3  \\\\
                &= 26 - 6 \\\\
                &= 20 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Width}    &= 17 - 2 \times 3  \\\\
                &= 17 - 6 \\\\
                &= 11 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Height}   &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Volume}   &= 20 \times 11 \times 3 \\\\
                &= 660 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$660 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cube has side lengths of $5 \ \text{cm}$. What is the volume of the cube? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A cube has side lengths of $5 \ \text{cm}$. What is the volume of the cube? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Volume}            &= 5 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
                          &= 125 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$125 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A second cube has a volume of $27 \ \text{cm}^3$. 

What is the total surface area of the second cube?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A second cube has a volume of $27 \ \text{cm}^3$. 
<emptyline>
What is the total surface area of the second cube?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Surface area of cube} = 6 \times \text{edge} \times \text{edge}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the edge of cube $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned} 
a^3                             &= 27 \\\\
a                               &= \sqrt[3]{27} \\\\
                                &= \sqrt[3]{3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                                &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Surface area}             &= 6a^2 \\\\ 
                                &= 6 \times 3^2 \\\\
                                &= 6 \times 3^2 \\\\
                                &= 6 \times 9 \\\\ 
                                &= 54 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

Surface area $= 54 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

<emptyline>

$
\begin{aligned} 
a^3                             &= 27 \\\\
a                               &= \sqrt[3]{27} \\\\
                                &= \sqrt[3]{3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                                &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Surface area}             &= 6a^2 \\\\ 
                                &= 6 \times 3^2 \\\\
                                &= 6 \times 3^2 \\\\
                                &= 6 \times 9 \\\\ 
                                &= 54 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Surface area $= 54 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subsubquestion">
<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the volume of the shape?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the volume of the shape?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE



*TODO:: Add an image with the shape rectangular cuboid by adding small cube in the given shape*


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


We can add small cube in the shape in a way such that it form cuboid.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Volume of the shape}  &= \text {Volume of cuboid} - \text {Volume of added cubes} \\\\
                             &= 1 \times 4 \times 4 - 6 \times 1 \times 1 \times 1 \\\\
                             &= 16 - 6 \\\\
                             &= 10 \ \text{cm} ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 \ \text{cm} ^ 3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subsubquestion">
<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many small cubes must be added to make a large cube with each side $4 \ \text{cm}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many small cubes must be added to make a large cube with each side $4 \ \text{cm}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of rectangular box} } { \text{Volume of cube} } \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 4 \times 4} {1 \times 1 \times 1} \\\\
                       &= 64
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$64$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the largest number of small cubes which could fit into the bottom layer of the box?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the largest number of small cubes which could fit into the bottom layer of the box?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

- ABBR: $\text{Area} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes fit into the bottom layer}  &= \dfrac { \text{surface area of bottom layer of the box} } { \text{area of one layer of cube} } \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 8} {2 \times 2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {32} {4} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                       &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_square
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 8} {2 \times 2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {32} {4} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                       &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the greatest number of small cubes which could fit into the box?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the greatest number of small cubes which could fit into the box?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of rectangular box}} {\text{Volume of cube}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 8 \times 6 } {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 2 \times 4 \times 6} {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times 4 \times 6} {2 \times 2 \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
                       &= 4 \times 6 \\\\
                       &= 24
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes} &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of rectangular box}} {\text{Volume of cube}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 8 \times 6 } {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 2 \times 4 \times 6} {2 \times 2 \times 2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times 4 \times 6} {2 \times 2 \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
                       &= 4 \times 6 \\\\
                       &= 24
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Robert has $70$ identical cube bricks.

He uses some of his bricks to make this cuboid: 

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2012/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2012/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2012/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png")

From his remaining bricks, he uses some to make the largest cube that he can.

How many bricks does he use to make the cube?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Robert has $70$ identical cube bricks.
<emptyline>
He uses some of his bricks to make this cuboid: 
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
From his remaining bricks, he uses some to make the largest cube that he can.
<emptyline>
How many bricks does he use to make the cube?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$

- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Bricks used to make cuboid}   &= 4 \times 4 \times 2 \\\\
                                    &= 32 \\\\
\text{Bricks left}                  &= 70 - 32 \\\\
                                    &= 38 \\\\
\text{Maximum length of the cube}   &= \sqrt[3]{38} \\\\
                                    &\approx 3 \\\\
\text{Bricks used for cube}         &= 3 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\
                                    &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Bricks used to make cuboid}   &= 4 \times 4 \times 2 \\\\
                                    &= 32 \\\\
\text{Bricks left}                  &= 70 - 32 \\\\
                                    &= 38 \\\\
\text{Maximum length of the cube}   &= \sqrt[3]{38} \\\\
                                    &\approx 3 \\\\
\text{Bricks used for cube}         &= 3 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\
                                    &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$27$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes after making twice as long} &= 12\times 2 \\\\
                                                  &= 24
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes after making twice as wide} &= 24 \times 2 \\\\
                                                  &= 48
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes after making twice as high} &= 48 \times 2 \\\\ 
                                                  &= 96
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of cubes used by Tally more than Tilly} &= 96 - 12 \\\\ 
                                                     &= 84 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$84$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Cube $A$ has a volume of $8 \ \text{cm}^3$.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2016/group-1/s01-q38-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2016/group-1/s01-q38-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2016/group-1/s01-q38-i00.png") 


Another cube, $B$, has edges which are twice the length of the edges of cube $A$.

Work out the volume of cube $B$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Cube $A$ has a volume of $8 \ \text{cm}^3$.
<emptyline>
%image% 
<emptyline>

Another cube, $B$, has edges which are twice the length of the edges of cube $A$.
<emptyline>
Work out the volume of cube $B$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the side of cube A $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a^3                     &= 8 \\\\
\text{Volume of cube B} &= (2a)^3 \\\\
                        &= 8a^3 \\\\
                        &= 8 \times 8 \\\\
                        &= 64 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$64 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A child's building brick 
measures $2 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $3 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $4 \ \text{centimetres}$. 
What is the greatest number of these bricks which can be packed into a 
box which measures $16 \ \text{centimetres}$ 
by $15 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $10 \ \text{centimetres}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A child's building brick 
measures $2 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $3 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $4 \ \text{centimetres}$. 
What is the greatest number of these bricks which can be packed into a 
box which measures $16 \ \text{centimetres}$ 
by $15 \ \text{centimetres}$ by $10 \ \text{centimetres}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bricks} &= \dfrac { \text{Volume of box} } { \text{Volume of a brick} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac { 16 \times 15 \times 10  } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac { 4 \times 2 \times 2 \times 3 \times 5 \times 10  } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac { \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} \times 2 \times \cancel{3} \times 5 \times 10  } { \cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} } \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 5 \times 10   \\\\
                        &= 100
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bricks} &= \dfrac { \text{Volume of box} } { \text{Volume of a brick} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac { 16 \times 15 \times 10  } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac { 4 \times 2 \times 2 \times 3 \times 5 \times 10  } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac { \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} \times 2 \times \cancel{3} \times 5 \times 10  } { \cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} } \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 5 \times 10   \\\\
                        &= 100
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$100$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


<div class="review">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Tim puts bricks which are all $2 \ \text{cm}$ by $3 \ \text{cm}$ by $4 \ \text{cm}$ into a box 
which is $15 \ \text{cm}$ by $20 \ \text{cm}$ by $16 \ \text{cm}$. 

What is the greatest number of bricks he can put in the box?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Tim puts bricks which are all $2 \ \text{cm}$ by $3 \ \text{cm}$ by $4 \ \text{cm}$ into a box 
which is $15 \ \text{cm}$ by $20 \ \text{cm}$ by $16 \ \text{cm}$. 
<emptyline>
What is the greatest number of bricks he can put in the box?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bricks}  &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the box}} {\text{Volume of the brick}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {15  \times 20 \times 16 } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 }  \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 3  \times 20 \times 4 \times 2 \times 2 } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 }  \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel{3}  \times 20 \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} \times 2 } { \cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} } \\\\
                         &= 5 \times 20 \times 2  \\\\
                         &= 200
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$200$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bricks}  &= \dfrac {\text{Volume of the box}} {\text{Volume of the brick}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {15  \times 20 \times 16 } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 }  \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 3  \times 20 \times 4 \times 2 \times 2 } { 2 \times 3 \times 4 }  \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel{3}  \times 20 \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{2} \times 2 } { \cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} } \\\\
                         &= 5 \times 20 \times 2  \\\\
                         &= 200
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$200$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two metal weights in the form of cuboids have the same volume.  One is $20 \ \text{cm}$ long, $16 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $9 \ \text{cm}$ high. 
The other weight is $10 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $12 \ \text{cm}$ high. 

What is its length if the volume of a cuboid is given by $\text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two metal weights in the form of cuboids have the same volume.  One is $20 \ \text{cm}$ long, $16 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $9 \ \text{cm}$ high. 
The other weight is $10 \ \text{cm}$ wide and $12 \ \text{cm}$ high. 
<emptyline>
What is its length if the volume of a cuboid is given by $\text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the second cuboid (in $\text{cm}$) $= l$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times 12 \times l   &=20 \times 16 \times 9 \\\\
l                       &= \dfrac {20 \times 16 \times 9} {10 \times 12} \\\\
l                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \times 4 \times 4 \times 3 \times 3} {10 \times 4 \times 3} \\\\
l                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 1\cancel{0} \times \cancel{4} \times 4 \times \cancel{3} \times 3} {1\cancel{0} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
l                       &= 2 \times 4 \times 3 \\\\
l                       &= 24 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length of the second cuboid $= 24 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the length of the second cuboid (in $\text{cm}$) $= l$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times 12 \times l   &=20 \times 16 \times 9 \\\\
l                       &= \dfrac {20 \times 16 \times 9} {10 \times 12} \\\\
l                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \times 4 \times 4 \times 3 \times 3} {10 \times 4 \times 3} \\\\
l                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 1\cancel{0} \times \cancel{4} \times 4 \times \cancel{3} \times 3} {1\cancel{0} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
l                       &= 2 \times 4 \times 3 \\\\
l                       &= 24 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Length of the second cuboid $= 24 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$24 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a set of cuboids, the three measurements, length, width and height are always in the ratio $5 : 4 : 3$

i.e.  Ratio of $\text{Length} : \text{Width} : \text{Height} = 5 : 4 : 3$ for each of the cuboids.

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The **volume** of a sphere can be calculated approximately using the following formula:

$V = 4r^3$ which means $4 \times r \times r \times r$, where $r$ is the radius of the sphere.

The **mass** of the sphere is then calculated using the formula:

$M = \dfrac{V \times d}{1000}$  where $d$ is the density of the material that makes up the sphere.

For example if $r = 20$ and $d = 3$ then we can find the Volume $(V)$ and the Mass $(M)$ as follows:

$V = 4 \times (20)^3 = 4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20 = 32000$

and $M = \dfrac{32000 \times 3}{1000} = 96$

Using the **two** formulae:

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
The **mass** of the sphere is then calculated using the formula:
<emptyline>
$M = \dfrac{V \times d}{1000}$  where $d$ is the density of the material that makes up the sphere.
<emptyline>
For example if $r = 20$ and $d = 3$ then we can find the Volume $(V)$ and the Mass $(M)$ as follows:
<emptyline>
$V = 4 \times (20)^3 = 4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20 = 32000$
<emptyline>
and $M = \dfrac{32000 \times 3}{1000} = 96$
<emptyline>
Using the **two** formulae:
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Bilal has made a box in the shape of a cuboid with sides $4 \ \text{cm}, 5 \ \text{cm}$ and $32 \ \text{cm}$. 
He wants to make another **different** shaped box but with the **same** volume. 
This box will have a height of $10 \ \text{cm}$ and a square base. What will be the length of the base?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Bilal has made a box in the shape of a cuboid with sides $4 \ \text{cm}, 5 \ \text{cm}$ and $32 \ \text{cm}$. 
He wants to make another **different** shaped box but with the **same** volume. 
This box will have a height of $10 \ \text{cm}$ and a square base. What will be the length of the base?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cuboid} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of the base (in $\text{cm}$) $= a$

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times a \times a    &= 4 \times 5 \times 32 \\\\
a^{2}                   &= \dfrac {4 \times 5 \times 32} {10} \\\\
a^{2}                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 32} {2 \times 5} \\\\
a^{2}                   &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{5} \times 32} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
a^{2}                   &= 2 \times 32 \\\\
a^{2}                   &= 64 \\\\
a                       &= \sqrt{64} \\\\
a                       &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length of the base $= 8 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cuboid
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

a^{2}                   &= 2 \times 32 \\\\

a                       &= \sqrt{64} \\\\
a                       &= 8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Length of the base $= 8 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Another tower is built with $72\, \text{centimetre cubes}$. What could be the length, height and width of this tower?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Another tower is built with $72\, \text{centimetre cubes}$. What could be the length, height and width of this tower?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Lets assume the same width and the length of the tower

Let the height of tower $= h$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume}            &= 72 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
3 \times 2 \times h      &= 72 \\\\
h                        &= \dfrac{72}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {12 \times 3 \times 2}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{3} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
                         &= 12 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$

Height of the tower $= 12 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \ \text{cm}, 2 \ \text{cm}, 12 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Lets assume the same width and the length of the tower
<emptyline>
Let the height of tower $= h$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume}            &= 72 \ \text{cm}^3 \\\\
3 \times 2 \times h      &= 72 \\\\
h                        &= \dfrac{72}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {12 \times 3 \times 2}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{3} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
                         &= 12 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Height of the tower $= 12 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The total surface area, $T$ , of a solid hemisphere is connected to its volume, $V$ , by the formula $4\,T^3 = 243\pi\,V^2$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The total surface area, $T$ , of a solid hemisphere is connected to its volume, $V$ , by the formula $4\,T^3 = 243\pi\,V^2$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If $V = 240\,\text{cm}^{3}$ , find $T$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If $V = 240\,\text{cm}^{3}$ , find $T$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
4T^3            &= 243 \pi V^2 \\\\
4T^3            &= 243 \times 3.14 \times 240^2 \\\\
4T^3            &= 763.02 \times 240^2 \\\\
T^3             &= \dfrac{763.02 \times 240^2}{4} \\\\
T^3             &= \dfrac{763.02 \times 240 \times 4 \times 60}{4} \\\\
T^3             &= \dfrac{763.02 \times 240 \times \cancel 4 \times 60}{\cancel 4} \\\\
T^3             &= 763.02 \times 240 \times 60 \\\\
T               &= \sqrt[3] {763.02 \times 240 \times 60 } \\\\
T               &= 222.31
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$222.31$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In an experiment, $T$ is measured as $30\, \text{cm}^{2}$ and $V$ as $12\, \text{cm}^{3}$ . What value of $\pi$  does the formula then give?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In an experiment, $T$ is measured as $30\, \text{cm}^{2}$ and $V$ as $12\, \text{cm}^{3}$ . What value of $\pi$  does the formula then give?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
4T^3                                       &= 243 \pi V^2 \\\\
4 \times (20)^3                            &= 243 \times \pi \times 12^2 \\\\
4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20            &= 243 \times \pi \times 12 \times 12 \\\\
243 \times \pi \times 12 \times 12         &= 4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20 \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac { 4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20} {243 \times 12 \times 12} \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac { 4 \times 4 \times 5 \times 20 \times 20} {243 \times 4 \times 3 \times 4 \times 3} \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac { \cancel 4 \times \cancel 4 \times 5 \times 20 \times 20} {243 \times \cancel4 \times 3 \times \cancel 4 \times 3} \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac {2000} {243 \times 9} \\\\
\pi                                        &= 0.91
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0.91$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$

243 \times \pi \times 12 \times 12         &= 4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20 \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac { 4 \times 20 \times 20 \times 20} {243 \times 12 \times 12} \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac { 4 \times 4 \times 5 \times 20 \times 20} {243 \times 4 \times 3 \times 4 \times 3} \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac { \cancel 4 \times \cancel 4 \times 5 \times 20 \times 20} {243 \times \cancel4 \times 3 \times \cancel 4 \times 3} \\\\
\pi                                        &= \dfrac {2000} {243 \times 9} \\\\
\pi                                        &= 0.91
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If the values of $T$ and $V$ are numerically the same, find $T$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the values of $T$ and $V$ are numerically the same, find $T$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the value of T $=t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4T^3                                       &= 243 \pi V^2 \\\\
4t^3                                       &= 243 \pi t^2 \\\\
4t \times t^2                              &= 243 \pi t^2 \\\\
t                                          &= \dfrac{243 \pi t^2} {4 \times t^2} \\\\
t                                          &= \dfrac{243 \pi \times \cancel {t^2}} {4 \times \cancel {t^2}} \\\\
t                                          &= \dfrac{243 \pi} {4} \\\\
t                                          &= \dfrac{243 \times 3.14} {4} \\\\
t                                          &= 190 .755
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$190.755$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram represents a carton of orange juice in the shape of a cuboid. The carton is full. 
![papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png](/assets/papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png "papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram represents a carton of orange juice in the shape of a cuboid. The carton is full. 
%image%
</code></pre>
